STARHAVEN — NOTABLE PLACES IMAGE PROMPTS (for Grok image generation)
========================================================================

Still to generate: 632   (finished so far: 20)

HOW TO USE
- Size: 1536 x 1024 pixels, horizontal 3:2 (landscape). In Grok pick the landscape / 3:2 aspect.
- Save each image as JPG into  starhaven_places_image/  with the exact file name shown after "Save as".
- Places whose image already exists are left out of this file automatically.

Rayden built it after Starhold rose, for a promise made to Selene Voss as she died in the snow at Fort Asterfall: somewhere nobody needed swords. Setting context: The capital of Starhaven — built from the ground up after the founding.

The Dawnfleet operates from its harbor. No naval force has successfully breached the cliff approaches. The fleet launches and returns through the same three-mile channel, entirely covered by fortress cannon. The cliff fortress was not designed by a military architect.

Admiral Valtor Crane's arrangement: every allied fleet checks into Saltmere before any other port.

What it is: The ruins of the pre-Starhaven settlement consumed by a Void event in Year 0 — one decade before the kingdom's founding. Preserved under a Veil Watch containment seal that has held for 400 years. Strictly guided scholarly access. The Frozen Market — a street where Void alteration stopped all organic matter mid-motion — is the most documented and least revisited feature. Scholars who study the Frozen Market once rarely request second access.

Deans: Talia Merrow (Medicine), Imara Kesh (Engineering), Lynet Vexley (Natural Sciences), Halden Quist (Mathematics), Ysra Callow (Law), Bastian Orrel (Architecture), Aldren Hander (History and Political Theory), Corvan Lisk (Trade and Commerce); Selin Aurth keeps the Observatory. The only institution that formally accepts foreign students for a published fee. Its graduates staff hospitals, build bridges, and serve as technical officers in the Engineering Corps. The Arcanum and Learnstead have a formal referral agreement since Year 1038: arcane-ability applicants go to the Arcanum, non-magical applicants go to Learnstead.

No text, no signage lettering, no watermark, no border, no logo. Subject: Skyperch Landing — District (Starhold — Eastern Cliff Plateau). What it is: The aerial operations district built into a cliff-face plateau east of Starhold — permanent base of the Stormriders and the Stormwing Brotherhood. Griffin roost halls, eagle perches, and the Altitude Record Board. The altitude record board lists Tessa Skarne at 14,000 feet on griffin-back. Any griffins shown are war griffins drawn as true griffins — eagle head, feathered neck, great feathered wings and taloned eagle forelegs, lion body, lion hind legs and a tufted lion tail; tawny, grey or white plumage; no scales, never a dragon. Any eagles shown are giant war eagles big enough to carry a rider, with a wingspan of thirty feet or more — real eagle anatomy, hooked beak, golden or dark plumage.

Their first real payment went toward a room above the Rusted Lantern, an upper-floor tavern overlooking the eastern canal district.

No text, no signage lettering, no watermark, no border, no logo. Subject: Deepforge — Historic site (Beneath the mountains — the old dwarven frontier). What it is: An ancient dwarven forge-city buried beneath the mountains — forge halls on colossal rune-carved pillars, broken bridges over dark rivers, old magma channels glowing under cracked grates. The dwarves sealed it long before the frontier collapsed. In Year 1041 the founding party went in.
